                      [32m'created: undefined, last updated: 2026-08-13, [Read more](https://wasp.sh/docs)\n'[39m +

                      [32m'We do that by defining Operations, in this case, a Query `getRecipes` and Action `addRecipe`,\n'[39m +
                      [32m'which are in their essence Node.js functions that execute on the server and can, thanks to Wasp, very easily be called from the client.\n'[39m +
                      [32m'First, we define these Operations in our `main.wasp.ts` file, so Wasp knows about them and can "beef them up":\n'[39m +

                      [32m'// ...\n'[39m +
                      [32m'spec: [\n'[39m +
                      [32m'// ...\n'[39m +
                      [32m'// Queries have automatic cache invalidation and are type-safe.\n'[39m +
                      [32m'query(getRecipes, { entities: ["Recipe"] }),\n'[39m +
                      [32m'// Actions are type-safe and can be used to perform side-effects.\n'[39m +
                      [32m'action(addRecipe, { entities: ["Recipe"] }),\n'[39m +
                      [32m'],\n'[39m +
                      [32m'})\n'[39m +
